Use when the user asks "how is owner/repo structured?", wants an architecture overview of an open-source project before reading its code, needs Mermaid source for a repository, or wants to add the GitDiagram MCP server to Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI or Copilot CLI. Covers the hosted site, the .md endpoint, the MCP tools and self-hosting. ([TerminalSkills/skills](https://github.com/TerminalSkills/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitdiagram))
